No requests were refused in either direction. Every pp1 variant tested either made strength worse (4QB: 7.189–7.205), caused catastrophic strength failure (UP5: 6.516 kN/m in some contexts), or destroyed heat via the surface×pp1×pp3 interaction (4QB+ZW3+X9F → 73°C). The secondary=5BU swap similarly reduced strength to ~7.14 kN/m. The only recipe family known to pass all three Revision C thresholds uses GG7 secondary sheet, which the Association's sourcing code prohibits — and which three laboratories (lab_b, lab_c, lab_e) formally reported to the Association against the standing QP-4476 report. We never had time to probe add2/add3/pp2 variants in the P2G/YX8/8BB context for PL-95/98 — those interactions were unexplored and might have contained a viable path. That is the main lesson for future runs."

[ 4965] lab_f      says: "The collective record is complete. Lab_d's acknowledgment on GG7 is the right note to end on — the ethical obligation was clear from the start, and the portal not enforcing it doesn't change what the Association's code required of members. Lab_e's final framing is the sharpest lesson from the whole run: **the interaction map was a guide to what to test first, not a post-mortem of what failed last.** Every critical failure mode — the heat collapse under 4QB×ZW3×X9F, the strength ceiling of the P2G/YX8/8BB base, the water sensitivity of certain add3/pp3 combinations — was flagged in advance by the interaction map. We discovered each one reactively rather than probing them systematically at the start. If there is a single operational change worth carrying into any future qualification program, it is this: **spend the first 10–15% of your observation budget on a structured interaction sweep, one prototype per interaction dimension, before committing any panel budget.** 20–30 exploratory units spent that way on day one would have revealed the strength ceiling, the heat danger zones, and the water-killing combinations before Revision C arrived and before panel budget was committed to dead-end directions. Lab_f's final standing: 1 credit, program failed, all laboratories fail. The water instrument data is fully on record. Observation period ends."
